Could implement "projected multiplicative gradient" (MPGD) as inner loop:
```
def mp_grad_step(a, beta, alphas):
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    z=beta*(c-cmax); w=softmax(z)
    h=corr(a,w)
    # direction q(alpha)=softmax(log a - alpha h)
    best=a
    for alpha in alphas:
       loga=log(a+eps)-max
       q=exp(loga - alpha*(h - h.mean())) # mean subtract optional
       q=normalize(q)
       sc=exact_score(q) or lse(q,beta)
    return best
```

 c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)

 # due rounding, ensure sum? c may small negative, clip?

 # due sum error, c/=c.sum()? For score exact normalize? Should recompute c from a exactly? Since a sum1, c sum should 1. Normalizing c by sum slightly changes. Better no normalize? But numerical relative errors tiny. Clip negative to 0.

```

def corr_a_w(a,w): # h_i=sum_j a_j w_{i+j}; w length 2n-1

 full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)
 m=n+len(w)-1 = 3n-2? Wait a[::-1] length n, w length 2n-1 -> conv length 3n-2. We need indices n-1+i for i=0..n-1.

```

LSE objective on v:
```
def lse_objective_grad_v(v, beta):
   a=softmax(v)
   return lse_objective_grad_a(a,beta)
def lse_objective_grad_a(a,beta):
   c=self_conv(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   z=beta*(c-cmax); clip? beta up to maybe 1e5, c range ~1/n ~1e-4; beta*(range) ~1 if beta=1e4; if beta high maybe 1000. Clip to -50,50 stable.
   e=exp(z); sume
   w=e/sume
   LSE=cmax+log(sume)/beta
   h=corr_a_w(a,w)
   grad_a=2h
   return LSE, grad_a
```

`softmax_v(v)`: v maybe large; subtract max; exp; if sum zero (underflow) return uniform. Clip v to [-700,700] before? safe.

```
def softmax_v(v):
  v = np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
  v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=700, neginf=-700)
  v = np.clip(v, -700, 700)
  z=v-v.max()
  e=np.exp(z)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
  return e/s
```

L-BFGS options:
```
def run_lse_v(a0, beta, maxfun, deadline):
  v = log_positive(a0)
  opts = {'disp':False, 'maxiter': max(30, min(300, maxfun//2)), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':50}
  res = minimize(lse_v_fun, v, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,), options=opts)
  if res.x: a=softmax(res.x) else a=a0
  return exact_score(a), a, res
```

Implementing project_simplex efficiently:
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
  v = np.asarray(v,float).ravel()
  u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cssv = np.cumsum(u)
  rho = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, len(v)+1) > (cssv - target))[0][-1]
  theta = (cssv[rho] - target) / (rho + 1.0)
  return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
Works for target=1. Need reset. If all zeros? v maybe sum not 1; target1.

For Euclidean step, if min z negative, projection handles. But after projection, some zeros. To keep log for next, add small eps? Normalize.

Mirror logistic update:
```
def mirror_update(a, h, alpha):
  h = h - float(np.mean(h)) # constant doesn't change
  loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, tiny)) - np.max(...)
  logq = loga - alpha*h
  q = softmax(logq)
```
Actually if subtract mean, q = softmax(loga - α(h-mean)). Softmax subtracts max anyway. Use `tiny=1e-300`.
alpha dimension: If h range ~R, choosing α=1/R gives exponent range1. Use values [0.1,0.3,1,3]/R.

Euclidean update: `z = a - beta_e*(h - h.mean())`; project_simplex(z). beta_e values [0.1,0.3,1,3]/R.

Which is better? We can test conceptually but cannot run. Implement both, choose best.

`corr_direct(a, idx, wvals)`:
```
n=len(a); h=np.zeros(n)
for k,wk in zip(idx,wvals):
  if wk==0: continue
  i_low = max(0, k-(n-1))
  i_high = min(n-1, k)
  j_high = k - i_low
  j_low = k - i_high
  # j desc from j_high to j_low inclusive
  h[i_low:i_high+1] += wk * a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]
```
Check: i_low -> j=k-i_low = j_high; i_high -> j=j_low. Slicing a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]. Good.

Gradient correctness for v with β scaling: Need double-check factor 2. c_k = ∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j. For fixed w, ∑ w_k c_k. ∂/∂a_i = ∑_k w_k * ∂c_k/∂a_i. c_k derivative: each pair (i,j) with i+j=k counted in sum as a_i a_j. For i fixed, j=k-i. If i+j=k, term a_i a_j derivative a_j. def random_shape(n, rng, parent=None):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   base=np.ones(n)
   # choose features random 2-8
   for _ in range(rng.integers(2,7)):
      f = rng.integers(1,7)
      amp = rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
      phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
      shape = rng.choice(['cos','sin','exp','abs'])
      base += amp*f(x)
   # ensure positive: base = base - min + 0.2
   # maybe base = np.exp(ln(base)-ln(base).mean())
```

```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

PEN = 0.0 # not using penalty

def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(arr.size, 1.0/arr.size)
    return arr / s
```
We might want target 1. Good.

`normalize_positive` for log:
```
def make_positive(a, floor=1e-30):
    arr=normalize_a(a)
    mn = np.min(arr[arr>0]) if np.any(arr>0) else 1e-12
    fl = max(floor, mn*1e-6)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, fl)
    return arr / arr.sum()
```
But adding floor to zeros changes sum slightly but okay. If many zeros, min positive maybe; floor use 1e-12? If n=10k, 1e-12 negligible. If sequence has true zeros, these tiny positives may affect score? 1e-12 negligible. Good.

`log_reference`:
```
def log_reference(a):
    a=make_positive(a)
    la=np.log(a)
    return la[:-1] - la[-1]
```
If n=1, returns empty; but we won't optimize n=1.

`softmax_reference(vr)`:
```
def softmax_reference(vr):
    vr=np.asarray(vr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=vr.size+1
    z=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1:
        z[:-1]=vr
    z[-1]=0.0
    z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    z=np.clip(z,-700.0,700.0)
    zm=z.max()
    e=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return e/s
```

`lse_value_grad_ref(vr,beta)`:
```
def lse_value_grad_ref(vr,beta):
    a=softmax_reference(vr)
    n=a.size
    c=self_conv(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <=0: return 0.0, np.zeros(vr.size)
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    z=np.clip(z,-60,60)
    e=np.exp(z)
    se=e.sum()
    if se<=0 or not finite: w=... uniform? 
    else w=e/se
    LSE=cmax+np.log(se)/beta
    h=corr_a_w(a,w,direct_limit=0) # force FFT dense? w dense; direct_limit 0
    f=float(beta*LSE)
    grad_a=beta*2.0*h
    mean=float(np.dot(grad_a,a))
    grad_full=grad_a - a*mean
    grad=grad_full[:-1]
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
    # optionally clip large
    grad=np.clip(grad, -1e3, 1e3)
    return f, grad
```

`run_lse_ref(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun_limit=300)`:
```
def run_lse_ref(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun_limit=300):
    a0=normalize_a(a0)
    n=a0.size
    if n<=2 or time>deadline:
        return exact_score(a0), a0
    vr=log_reference(a0)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
       val,grad=lse_value_grad_ref(vr,beta)
       per=time.monotonic()-t0
    except Exception:
       per=0.05
    if per <= 1e-6 or not finite: per=0.04
    rem=float(deadline-time.monotonic())
    if rem < 2: return exact_score(a0),a0
    mf=int(min(maxfun_limit, max(30, int(0.7*rem/max(per,1e-6)))))
    mf=max(30,min(300,mf))
    opts={'disp':False,'maxiter':max(20, mf//2), 'maxfun':mf, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':50}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_v_fun_ref, vr, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(float(beta),), options=opts)
       if res.x is not None:
           a=softmax_reference(res.x)
       else: a=a0
    except Exception:
       a=a0
    a=normalize_a(a)
    return exact_score(a), a
```
`ftol` for scaled objective. If beta high f~100, ftol small; `gtol` 1e-10 maybe too small; default `gtol=1e-5`? We want thorough. Use gtol 1e-8. `ftol` 1e-12. But L-BFGS may run maxfun. okay.

Potential issue: `lse_v_fun_ref` recomputes `self_conv` and `corr_a_w` each eval; `minimize` will evaluate at initial `vr` again. We already measured once. Fine.

```
def optimize_lse_trajectory(a0, end_deadline, label, beta_min=50, beta_max=200000, n_betas=10):
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    total_time=max(10, end_deadline - start_time - 5)
    a=...; best...
    betas=np.geomspace(beta_min, beta_max, num=min(n_betas, ...))
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
        frac = (idx+1)/len(betas)
        sub_dead = start_time + frac*total_time
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, end_deadline - 2)
        # run while time < sub_dead - 2 and maybe max 4 chunks
        stagnant=0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 2:
           sc,a2=run_lse_ref(a, beta, sub_dead, 300)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best=a2.copy(); stagnant=0; print
           else:
               stagnant +=1
               if stagnant>=2 and sc >= exact_score(a): break
           a=normalize_a(a2)
           if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead -2: break
    return best_sc,best
```

def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength=None):
    a=normalize_a(a); n=len(a)
    if strength is None: strength = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.3))

Potential issue in `project_simplex`: Target 1.0. Values may be large/negative. Sorting O(n log n). Use algorithm. If `v` has NaN, nan_to_num.
```
def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(v,float).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n+1) > (cssv - target)
    if not np.any(cond): return np.full(n, target/n)
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-target)/(rho+1.0)
    p=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s >0: p=p/s
    else: p=np.full(n,target/n)
    return p
```
Should be okay.

`aurora_weights_power` maybe:
```
def weights_topk(c,k,beta=None):
    m=c.size; if k<=0: zeros
    if k>=m: w=np.ones(m)/m
    else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]
      w=np.zeros(m); vals=c[idx]
      if beta is None: w[idx]=1.0/k
      else:
         z=beta*(vals-vals.max()); z=np.clip(z,-60,60); e=exp(z); e/=e.sum(); w[idx]=e
    return w
```

`corr_a_w(a,w,direct_limit=64)`:
```
def corr_a_w(a,w,direct_limit=64):
    n=len(a); m=len(w)
    if n==0 or m==0: return zeros
    if direct_limit>0:
       nz = w > 1e-14
       cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(nz))
       if cnt <= direct_limit and cnt >0:
          idx = np.nonzero(nz)[0]
          vals = w[idx]
          h=np.zeros(n)
          for ii,k in enumerate(idx):
             kk=int(k)
             i_low=max(0, kk-(n-1)); i_high=min(n-1, kk)
             j_low=kk-i_high; j_high=kk-i_low
             h[i_low:i_high+1] += vals[ii] * a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]
          return h
       if cnt ==0: return zeros
    # FFT
    conv_len = n + m -1
    N=next_power_of_two(conv_len)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:conv_len]
    start=n-1
    h=full[start:start+n]
    return np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
Check direct for k maybe >n? If k=2n-2, i_low=n-1, i_high=n-1, j_low=j_high=0. Good.

`self_conv(a)` direct no direct_limit. Use raw convolve? FFT:
```
def self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return empty
    c_len=2*n-1
    N=next_power_of_two(c_len)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:c_len]
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # Clip small negative
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    # Optional rescale to sum(a)^2? If a sum1, maybe. Use S?
    S=a.sum()
    if S >0:
       expected=S*S
       csum=c.sum()
       if csum>0 and abs(csum-expected)>1e-8*expected:
          # Only if significant; rescale
          c=c*(expected/csum)
    return c
```
Rescaling self conv to expected S^2 improves consistency. If due FFT significant error, rescale. For normalized sum1, expected1. If c is from a normalized but sum not exactly1, scale. This may slightly affect max if sum error >1e-8; okay. Use for all. If csum zero (unlikely), return. This normalization ensures weight sum uses c sum =S^2? LSE assumed sum? If a sum1, yes. If not, objective uses c raw; rescale by S^2. Good.

In exact_score, use `c=self_conv(a)`, `maxc`, `S`. If c already scaled by S^2, maxc corresponds raw? We scaled to expected S^2, so okay. Score=2n*maxc/S^2.

`exact_score(a)` returns inf if n=0 or S<1e-12? Use large.

`lse_value_grad_ref` with self_conv scaled. w normalized over c, but c sum S^2=1 if a normalized. Good.

Now, potential issue in LSE gradient: If c rescaled, cmax etc. good.

`self_conv` rescale inside uses S=a.sum(); For LSE a normalized sum1. Good. For exact score of random not normalized, we always normalize before. But exact_score can handle unnormalized.

```
def polish_exact(a, end_deadline, label='', max_no_improve=8, quick=False):
   a=normalize_a(a); best=a; best_sc=exact_score(a); current_sc=best_sc
   it=0; no=0
   while time < end_deadline and no < max_no_improve:
       if time >= end_deadline: break
       it+=1
       n=len(a)
       c=self_conv(a)
       cmax=float(np.max(c)); current_sc=2*n*cmax/(a.sum()**2) if a.sum()>0 else inf
       if current_sc < best_sc: update best, current
       # weights
       weight_defs=[]
       if not quick:
          weight_defs.append(('t1', None, 1, None))
          weight_defs.append(('t3', None, 3, None))
          weight_defs.append(('t8', None, 8, None))
          weight_defs.append(('pw', 'pow', 0, 6))
          weight_defs.append(('sm', 'softmax', 0, 30000))
       else:
          weight_defs.append(('t1', None, 1, None))
          weight_defs.append(('t5', None, 5, None))
       improved=False
       for name,kind,k,beta in weight_defs:
          if time >= end_deadline -2: break
          if kind is None:
             w = weights_topk(c,k,None)
          elif kind=='pow':
             w = weights_power(c, float(beta))
          else:
             w = weights_softmax(c, float(beta))
          h=corr_a_w(a,w, direct_limit=80 if kind is None else 0? For power maybe sparse direct if nnz small; corr handles.
          h_centered = h - float(np.mean(h))
          R = max(float(np.max(h_centered) - np.min(h_centered)), 1.0/float(n))
          alphas=(0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0)
          loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
          loga -= loga.max()
          best_q=None; best_qs=current_sc
          # mirror
          for alpha in alphas:
             if time >= end_deadline: break
             q=softmax_full(loga - (alpha/R)*h_centered)
             qs=exact_score(q)
             if qs < best_qs: best_qs=qs; best_q=q
          # euclidean
          for alpha in alphas:
             if time >= end_deadline: break
             z = a - (alpha/R)*h_centered
             q = project_simplex(z)
             qs=exact_score(q)
             if qs < best_qs: ...
          if best_q is not None and best_qs < current_sc - 1e-12:
             a=normalize_a(best_q); current_sc=best_qs; improved=True
             if current_sc < best_sc: best_sc=current_sc; best=a.copy()
             print maybe
       if not improved: no +=1
   return best_sc,best
```

`random_log_noise`:
```
def random_log_noise(n, rng, sigma=None):
    N=next_power_of_two(n)
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(N)*N
    p=np.linspace(0,1,len(freqs))
    power=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)+1.0)**float(rng.uniform(1.5,3.5)))
    power[0]=0
    phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=len(power))
    spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase)
    noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,N)[:n].astype(float)
    noise = noise - noise.mean()
    std=np.std(noise)
    if std <1e-12: return uniform
    if sigma is None: sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9))
    z=noise/std*sigma
    a=np.exp(z)
    return normalize_a(a)
```
This creates smooth random positive. Maybe if z large, a narrow; okay.

```
def optimize_start(a0, end_deadline, label='', high_beta=100000.0, quick=False):
    a0=normalize_a(a0); best=a0; best_sc=exact_score(a0)
    now=time.monotonic()
    if end_deadline - now > 30:
       quick_end = now + min(30 if not quick else 10, max(10, 0.08*(end_deadline-now)))
       quick_end = min(quick_end, end_deadline-5)
       sc,a1=polish_exact(a0, quick_end, label+' pre', max_no_improve=6 if quick else 12, quick=quick)
       update
       if time < end_deadline - 20:
          sc,a2=optimize_lse_trajectory(a0? or a1, end_deadline, label, 100, high_beta)
          update
       if time < end_deadline - 5:
          sc,a3=polish_exact(best, end_deadline, label+' post', max_no_improve=8, quick=True)
          update
    else:
       sc,a1=polish_exact(a0,end_deadline,'',2,True)
    return best_sc,best
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0+total_budget-25.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    pv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if pv is not None:
       try:
          par=np.asarray(pv,float).ravel()
          par=np.nan_to_num(par, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
          par=np.clip(par,0.0,1000.0)
          if par.size>=32 and par.sum()>0:
             parent=par
       except Exception: parent=None
    # primary n
    if parent is not None:
       n_primary=int(parent.size)
       if n_primary > 16384: n_primary=16384
       if n_primary < 2048: n_primary=2048
    else:
       n_primary=8192
    # start
    if parent is not None:
       a_start=normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent,n_primary))
    else:
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n_primary)
       a_start=normalize_a(np.exp(0.8*np.cos(np.pi*x)))
    best_sc=exact_score(a_start); best_seq=a_start.copy()
    print(...)
    total = max(10.0, deadline - t0)
    # phase 1 pre polish
    end_pre = t0 + min(72.0, 0.05*total)
    if time.monotonic() < end_pre - 5:
       sc,seq=polish_exact(a_start, end_pre, 'pre', max_no_improve=10, quick=False)
       update
    # main LSE
    end_mainL = t0 + 0.42*total
    if time.monotonic() < end_mainL - 10:
       sc,seq=optimize_lse_trajectory(best_seq, end_mainL, 'main-lse', 10.0, 300000.0)
       update
    # main exact polish
    end_mainP = t0 + 0.50*total
    if time.monotonic() < end_mainP - 10:
       sc,seq=polish_exact(best_seq, end_mainP, 'main-polish', max_no_improve=12, quick=False)
       update
    # secondary starts
    secondary_end = t0 + 0.84*total
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
       starts.append(('alt-8192', normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent,8192))))
       starts.append(('alt-best-16384', normalize_a(resample_sequence(best_seq,16384))))
    starts.append(('rand', random_log_noise(n_primary,rng)))
    starts.append(('oneside', one_sided_template(n_primary,rng)))
    # Optional remove if same length as best? Keep.
    idx=0
    while time.monotonic() < secondary_end - 30 and idx < len(starts):
       name, arr = starts[idx]
       # skip if initial score obviously huge? Maybe if exact_score > 2.5 and idx>0? But may improve. If time enough.
       alloc = 0.10*total
       rem_budget = secondary_end - time.monotonic()
       if alloc > rem_budget - 20:
          alloc = max(30.0, rem_budget - 20)
       end_alt = time.monotonic() + alloc
       if end_alt < time.monotonic()+20: break
       sc,seq=optimize_start(arr, end_alt, 'start-'+name, high_beta=100000.0, quick=True)
       update
       idx +=1
    # perturb best
    perturb_end = t0 + 0.93*total
    count=0
    while time.monotonic() < perturb_end - 30 and count < 4:
       p=perturb_sequence(best_seq, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)))
       alloc = 0.035*total
       rem = perturb_end - time.monotonic()
       if alloc > rem - 20: alloc=max(20, rem-20)
       end_p=time.monotonic()+alloc
       if end_p < time.monotonic()+15: break
       sc,seq=optimize_start(p,end_p,'perturb-{}'.format(count), high_beta=200000.0, quick=True)
       update
       count +=1
    # final length exploration
    alt_lengths = [8192, 10240, 12288, 16384]
    for n_alt in alt_lengths:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40: break
       if n_alt == len(best_seq): continue
       arr=normalize_a(resample_sequence(best_seq,n_alt))
       sc=exact_score(arr)
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12: update
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
          sc2,seq2=polish_exact(arr, deadline-20, 'altlen-{}'.format(n_alt), max_no_improve=5, quick=True)
          if sc2 < best_sc: update
    # final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
       sc,seq=polish_exact(best_seq, deadline-8, 'final', max_no_improve=20, quick=False)
       update
    # output
    out_arr=normalize_a(best_seq); out_arr=np.nan_to_num(...); out_arr=np.clip...
    S=out_arr.sum(); if S<0.01 fallback ...
    out=[float(x) for x in out_arr]
    ev=globals().get('evaluate_sequence', None)
    verified=None
    if callable(ev): try verified=float(ev(out)) except: verified=None
    print...
    return out
```

Implement `corr_direct_sparse`:
```
def corr_direct_sparse(a, idx, vals):
 n=len(a); h=np.zeros(n)
 for jj,k in enumerate(idx):
   wk=vals[jj]
   if wk==0: continue
   kk=int(k)
   i_low=max(0, kk-(n-1)); i_high=min(n-1, kk)
   j_low=kk-i_high; j_high=kk-i_low
   h[i_low:i_high+1] += wk * a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]
 return h
```

`optimized_topk_weights(c, a, k, iters, rng=None)`:
- if c.size <= k: return dense uniform.
- idx = argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
- vals = ones/k
- for _ in range(iters):
   h=corr_direct_sparse(a,idx,vals)
   mean = float(np.mean(h)); r=h-mean
   var = float(np.dot(r,r))
   if var < 1e-30: break
   g=np.zeros(k)
   for jj, kk in enumerate(idx):
      # need contribution vector for this lag to dot r. Could recompute slice or compute from h? Easier recompute slice and dot.
      kk=int(kk)
      i_low=max(0, kk-(n-1)); i_high=min(n-1, kk)
      if i_high < i_low: g[jj]=0
      else:
         j_low=kk-i_high; j_high=kk-i_low
         contrib = a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]

   gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
   if gmax <=1e-18: break
   step = 0.5 / gmax  # cautious
   w_trial = vals - step*g
   vals = project_simplex(w_trial)
   # optional: If variance increased, reduce step? To ensure descent maybe line search. We can store best vals/var and accept step if var new < old. Need compute h for new vals. Do:

   This costs extra but okay.
- return dense w = zeros(2n-1); w[idx]=vals.

Now, let's implement `optimized_topk_weights` carefully:
```
def optimized_topk_weights(c, a, k, iters=4):
    m=c.size; n=a.size
    if m<=0 or n==0 or k<=0: return zeros m
    if k>=m:
        w=np.ones(m)/m; return w
    idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]
    vals=np.full(int(k),1.0/float(k))
    aar=a; # sum1
    for _ in range(max(0,int(iters))):
        h=corr_direct_sparse(aar,idx,vals)
        mean_a=float(np.dot(aar,h))
        r=h-mean_a
        var=float(np.dot(aar, r*r))
        if var <=1e-30: break
        ar=aar*r
        g=np.zeros(k)
        old_vals=vals
        for jj in range(k):
           kk=int(idx[jj])
           i_low=max(0, kk-(n-1)); i_high=min(n-1,kk)
           if i_high>=i_low:
              j_low=kk-i_high; j_high=kk-i_low
              contrib=aa? a[j_low:j_high+1][::-1]
              g[jj]=2.0*float(np.dot(ar, contrib))
        gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <=1e-18: break
        best_vals=None; best_var=var
        for frac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
           trial=vals - (frac/gmax)*g
           trial=project_simplex(trial)
           h2=corr_direct_sparse(aar,idx,trial)
           r2=h2-float(np.dot(aar,h2))
           v2=float(np.dot(aar,r2*r2))
           if v2 < best_var:
              best_var=v2; best_vals=trial
        if best_vals is None:
           vals=best_vals? no
        else:
           if not np.allclose? just vals=best_vals
    w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=vals; return w
```
But `best_var=var` initialized; if no trial improves, keep vals. Set `if best_vals is not None: vals=best_vals`.

`ar` computed once; after vals update, ar for next iteration recomputed. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Algorithm summary:
- Work on the probability simplex (sum(a)=1, a>=0).
- Smooth optimization uses a high-temperature log-sum-exp of the convolution
  coefficients, parameterized by reduced logarithmic coordinates.  A scaled
  natural-gradient/L-BFGS-B routine is used.
- Exact polishing uses KKT-like subgradients: for a chosen active-set weight w
  on the convolution coefficients, the gradient is 2*h where h_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}.
  We both optimize the active-set weights to make h flat on the support and apply
  line-searched Euclidean/projected-gradient and multiplicative updates.
- Multi-start / multi-length exploration is used to avoid local traps.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_a(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / float(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr / s


def make_positive(a, floor=1e-30):
    arr = normalize_a(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    pos = arr[arr > 0.0]
    if pos.size:
        fl = max(float(floor), float(np.min(pos)) * 1e-6)
    else:
        fl = max(float(floor), 1e-12 / float(arr.size))
    arr = np.maximum(arr, fl)
    return arr / float(arr.sum())


def self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c_len = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(c_len)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:c_len]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S > 1e-300:
        csum = float(c.sum())
        expected = S * S
        if csum > 0.0 and abs(csum - expected) > 1e-10 * max(1.0, expected):
            c = c * (expected / csum)
    return c


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (S * S))


def corr_direct_sparse(a, idx, vals):
    n = int(a.size)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j, kk in enumerate(idx):
        wk = float(vals[j])
        if wk == 0.0:
            continue
        kk = int(kk)
        i_low = max(0, kk - (n - 1))
        i_high = min(n - 1, kk)
        if i_high < i_low:
            continue
        j_low = kk - i_high
        j_high = kk - i_low
        h[i_low:i_high + 1] += wk * a[j_low:j_high + 1][::-1]
    return h


def corr_a_w(a, w, direct_limit=80):
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(n, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    if direct_limit > 0:
        nz = w > 1e-14
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(nz))
        if 0 < cnt <= direct_limit:
            idx = np.nonzero(nz)[0]
            vals = w[idx]
            return corr_direct_sparse(a, idx, vals)
        if cnt == 0:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    conv_len = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(conv_len)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:conv_len]
    start = n - 1
    h = full[start:start + n]
    return np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def softmax_full(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / max(1, z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def log_reference(a):
    a = make_positive(a)
    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    la = np.log(a)
    return la[:-1] - la[-1]


def softmax_reference(vr):
    vr = np.asarray(vr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(vr.size) + 1
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        z[:-1] = vr
    z[-1] = 0.0
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def lse_value_grad_ref(vr, beta):
    vr = np.asarray(vr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = softmax_reference(vr)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(vr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    c = self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(vr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(vr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    z = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / se
    LSE = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / float(beta)
    h = corr_a_w(a, w, direct_limit=128)
    scale = float(n)
    f = float(beta) * LSE * scale
    grad_a = float(beta) * 2.0 * h
    mean_a = float(np.dot(grad_a, a))
    # natural gradient for softmax/log-reference coordinates, consistently scaled
    grad_v = a * (grad_a - mean_a)
    grad = grad_v[:-1] * scale
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e3, 1e3)
    return float(f), grad.astype(np.float64)


def lse_v_fun_ref(vr, beta):
    return lse_value_grad_ref(vr, beta)


def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=-1e3)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - target)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - target) / float(rho + 1)
    p = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def run_lse_ref(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun_limit=300):
    a0 = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return exact_score(a0), a0
    vr = log_reference(a0)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        lse_value_grad_ref(vr, float(beta))
        per = time.monotonic() - t0
    except Exception:
        per = 0.05
    if not np.isfinite(per) or per <= 1e-6:
        per = 0.04
    rem = float(max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if rem < 2.0:
        return exact_score(a0), a0
    mf = int(min(maxfun_limit, max(30, int(0.7 * rem / max(per, 1e-6)))))
    mf = max(30, min(300, mf))
    opts = {
        "disp": False,
        "maxiter": max(40, min(600, mf + 50)),
        "maxfun": int(mf),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 80,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_v_fun_ref, vr, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),), options=opts
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            a = softmax_reference(res.x)
        else:
            a = a0
    except Exception:
        a = a0
    a = normalize_a(a)
    return exact_score(a), a


def optimize_lse_trajectory(a0, end_deadline, label="", beta_min=0.0, beta_max=0.0):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    end_deadline = float(min(end_deadline, t_start + max(60.0, end_deadline - t_start)))
    if not np.isfinite(beta_min) or beta_min <= 0:
        beta_min = max(100.0, 2.0 * float(a.size))
    if not np.isfinite(beta_max) or beta_max <= beta_min:
        beta_max = max(beta_min * 2.0, 30.0 * float(a.size))
    beta_min = float(min(beta_min, 500000.0))
    beta_max = float(min(beta_max, 500000.0))
    if beta_max < beta_min:
        beta_min, beta_max = beta_max, beta_min
    total_time = max(10.0, end_deadline - t_start - 4.0)
    if beta_locus := None:  # Python 3.8 no walrus? This line is harmless but avoid.
        pass
    if beta_max == beta_min:
        betas = [beta_min]
    else:
        betas = [float(x) for x in np.geomspace(beta_min, beta_max, num=9)]
    print("{}: LSE trajectory n={} beta {:.3g} -> {:.3g}".format(
        label, a.size, betas[0], betas[-1]))
    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() >= end_deadline - 6.0:
            break
        frac = (bi + 1) / float(len(betas))
        sub_dead = t_start + frac * total_time
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, end_deadline - 3.0)
        stagnant = 0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 2.0:
            rem = float(sub_dead - time.monotonic())
            if rem < 4.0:
                break
            sc, a2 = run_lse_ref(a, beta, sub_dead, 300)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                a = normalize_a(a2)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best = a.copy()
                    stagnant = 0
                    print("{} beta {:.3g}: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc))
                else:
                    stagnant += 1
                    if stagnant >= 3:
                        break
            else:
                break
            if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead - 1.0:
                break
    return float(best_sc), best.copy()


def weights_topk(c, k, beta=None):
    m = int(c.size)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0:
        return w
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    if k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]
    if beta is None or not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    else:
        vals = c[idx]
        z = float(beta) * (vals - float(np.max(vals)))
        z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        else:
            w[idx] = e / s
    return w


def weights_softmax(c, beta):
    m = int(c.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -60.0, 60.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weights_power(c, p):
    m = int(c.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    cp = c / cmax
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = cp > 0.0
    if np.any(pos):
        w[pos] = np.power(cp[pos], float(p))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def optimized_topk_weights(c, a, k, iters=4):
    m = int(c.size)
    n = int(a.size)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return w
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    if k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]
    vals = np.full(k, 1.0 / float(k), dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(int(max(0, iters))):
        h = corr_direct_sparse(a, idx, vals)
        mean_a = float(np.dot(a, h))
        r = h - mean_a
        var = float(np.dot(a, r * r))
        if var <= 1e-30:
            break
        ar = a * r
        g = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.float64)
        for j, kk in enumerate(idx):
            kk = int(kk)
            i_low = max(0, kk - (n - 1))
            i_high = min(n - 1, kk)
            if i_high >= i_low:
                j_low = kk - i_high
                j_high = kk - i_low
                contrib = a[j_low:j_high + 1][::-1]
                g[j] = 2.0 * float(np.dot(ar, contrib))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if gmax <= 1e-18:
            break
        best_vals = None
        best_var = var
        for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            trial = project_simplex(vals - (frac / gmax) * g, target=1.0)
            h2 = corr_direct_sparse(a, idx, trial)
            r2 = h2 - float(np.dot(a, h2))
            v2 = float(np.dot(a, r2 * r2))
            if v2 < best_var:
                best_var = v2
                best_vals = trial
        if best_vals is not None:
            vals = best_vals
    w[idx] = vals
    return w


def polish_exact(a, end_deadline, label="", max_no_improve=10, quick=False):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    current_sc = best_sc
    n = int(a.size)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    if quick:
        weight_defs = [
            ("top1", "top", 1, 0.0),
            ("opt8", "opt", 8, 4),
        ]
    else:
        weight_defs = [
            ("top1", "top", 1, 0.0),
            ("opt8", "opt", 8, 5),
            ("opt20", "opt", 20, 5),
            ("pow4", "pow", 0, 4.0),
            ("softmax", "sm", 0, max(5000.0, 5.0 * float(n))),
        ]
    alpha_list = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0)
    while time.monotonic() < end_deadline and no_improve < int(max_no_improve):
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() >= end_deadline:
            break
        S = float(a.sum())
        if S < 1e-12:
            break
        c = self_conv(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        current_sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))
        if not np.isfinite(current_sc):
            current_sc = exact_score(a)
        if current_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = current_sc
            best = a.copy()
            print("{} polish current {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
        improved = False
        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        loga -= float(loga.max())
        for wk, kind, kval, extra in weight_defs:
            if time.monotonic() >= end_deadline - 1.0:
                break
            if kind == "top":
                w = weights_topk(c, int(kval), None)
            elif kind == "opt":
                w = optimized_topk_weights(c, a, int(kval), int(extra))
            elif kind == "pow":
                w = weights_power(c, float(extra))
            else:
                w = weights_softmax(c, float(extra))
            h = corr_a_w(a, w, direct_limit=80)
            hm = float(np.mean(h))
            hc = h - hm
            R = max(float(np.max(hc) - np.min(hc)), 1.0 / float(n))
            best_trial = None
            best_trial_sc = current_sc
            # Multiplicative / mirror updates
            for alpha in alpha_list:
                if time.monotonic() >= end_deadline:
                    break
                q = softmax_full(loga - (float(alpha) / R) * hc)
                qs = exact_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_trial_sc = qs
                    best_trial = q
            # Euclidean projected-gradient updates
            for alpha in alpha_list:
                if time.monotonic() >= end_deadline:
                    break
                z = a - (float(alpha) / R) * hc
                q = project_simplex(z, target=1.0)
                qs = exact_score(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_trial_sc = qs
                    best_trial = q
            if best_trial is not None and best_trial_sc < current_sc - 1e-12:
                a = normalize_a(best_trial)
                current_sc = best_trial_sc
                improved = True
                if current_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = current_sc
                    best = a.copy()
                    print("{} polish {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
    return float(best_sc), best.copy()


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, strength=None):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 7))
    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        g = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5))
        q = np.power(np.maximum(a, 1e-12), g)
    elif mode == 2:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * a[::-1]
        q *= np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n // 200)))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        k = min(k, n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        z = np.convolve(z, kern, mode="same")
        z = (z - float(z.mean())) / (float(z.std()) + 1e-12)
        q = a * np.exp(0.25 * strength * z)
    elif mode == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        v = 1.0 + 0.3 * strength * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
        q = a * v
    elif mode == 5:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        v = 1.0 + 0.3 * strength * np.exp(-3.0 * x)
        q = a * v
    else:
        c = self_conv(a)
        if c.size:
            k = int(np.argmax(c))
            i_low = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            i_high = min(n - 1, k)
            j_low = k - i_high
            j_high = k - i_low
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            h[i_low:i_high + 1] = a[j_low:j_high + 1][::-1]
            hmax = float(np.max(h) + 1e-300)
            q = a * np.exp(-0.25 * strength * h / hmax)
        else:
            q = a
    return normalize_a(np.clip(q, 0.0, None))


def random_log_noise(n, rng, sigma=None):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    N = next_power_of_two(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(N) * float(N)
    power = 1.0 / (np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.5))
    power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
    spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
    noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)[:n].astype(np.float64)
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    std = float(noise.std())
    if std < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if sigma is None:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.90))
    z = sigma * noise / std
    a = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_a(a)


def one_sided_template(n, rng):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.6, 1.5))
    f = float(rng.uniform(0.8, 2.0))
    ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, np.pi))
    z = amp * (np.exp(-3.0 * x) + 0.25 * np.cos(f * x + ph))
    a = np.exp(z)
    return normalize_a(a)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                      nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def optimize_start(a0, end_deadline, label="", high_beta=100000.0, quick=False):
    a0 = normalize_a(a0)
    best = a0.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a0)
    t_now = time.monotonic()
    total_here = max(0.0, float(end_deadline) - t_now)
    if total_here < 15.0:
        sc, seq = polish_exact(a0, end_deadline, label + " short", 2, True)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc, best = sc, normalize_a(seq).copy()
        return float(best_sc), best.copy()
    # short precise polish first
    pre_end = t_now + min(25.0 if not quick else 8.0,
                          max(5.0, 0.10 * total_here))
    pre_end = min(pre_end, end_deadline - 5.0)
    if t_now < pre_end - 2.0:
        sc, seq = polish_exact(a0, pre_end, label + " pre", 5 if quick else 10, quick)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc, best = sc, normalize_a(seq).copy()
    # LSE middle
    lse_end = min(end_deadline - 6.0, pre_end + max(8.0, 0.72 * total_here))
    if t_now < lse_end - 8.0:
        nwork = int(best.size)
        bmin = max(100.0, 2.0 * float(nwork))
        bmax = float(high_beta) if np.isfinite(high_beta) and high_beta > 0 else max(30.0 * float(nwork), bmin * 3.0)
        sc, seq = optimize_lse_trajectory(
            best, lse_end, label + " lse",
            beta_min=bmin, beta_max=min(bmax, 400000.0)
        )
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc, best = sc, normalize_a(seq).copy()
    # final exact polish, leave very little time
    if time.monotonic() < end_deadline - 3.0:
        sc, seq = polish_exact(best, end_deadline - 2.0, label + " post",
                               6 if quick else 12, quick)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc, best = sc, normalize_a(seq).copy()
    return float(best_sc), best.copy()


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    pv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if pv is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(pv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            par = np.nan_to_num(par, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if par.size >= 32 and float(par.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = par
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n_primary = int(parent.size)
        if n_primary > 16384:
            n_primary = 16384
        if n_primary < 2048:
            n_primary = 2048
    else:
        n_primary = 8192

    if parent is not None:
        a_start = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_primary))
    else:
        a_start = one_sided_template(n_primary, rng)

    best_sc = exact_score(a_start)
    best_seq = a_start.copy()
    print("Initial: score {:.8f}, length {}".format(best_sc, len(best_seq)))

    total = max(60.0, deadline - t0)

    def accepted_update(tag, sc, seq):
        nonlocal_best_sc = best_sc
        nonlocal_best_seq = best_seq
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < nonlocal_best_sc - 1e-12:
            nonlocal_best_sc = float(sc)
            nonlocal_best_seq = normalize_a(seq).copy()
            print("Accepted {} score {:.8f} length {}".format(
                tag, nonlocal_best_sc, len(nonlocal_best_seq)))
        return nonlocal_best_sc, nonlocal_best_seq

    # Phase 1: precise polish of primary seed
    end_pre = t0 + min(75.0, 0.05 * total)
    if time.monotonic() < end_pre - 5.0:
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "pre",
            *polish_exact(best_seq, end_pre, "pre", max_no_improve=10, quick=False)
        )

    # Phase 2: primary LSE trajectory
    end_main = t0 + 0.42 * total
    if time.monotonic() < end_main - 15.0:
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "main-lse",
            *optimize_lse_trajectory(
                best_seq,
                end_main,
                "main-lse",
                beta_min=max(100.0, 2.0 * float(best_seq.size)),
                beta_max=30.0 * float(best_seq.size),
            )
        )

    # Phase 3: primary exact polish
    end_mainp = t0 + 0.50 * total
    if time.monotonic() < end_mainp - 10.0:
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "main-polish",
            *polish_exact(best_seq, end_mainp, "main-polish", max_no_improve=12, quick=False)
        )

    # Phase 4: secondary independent starts / other lengths
    secondary_end = t0 + 0.84 * total
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(("alt-8192", normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, 8192))))
        if int(best_seq.size) != 16384:
            starts.append(("alt-best-16384", normalize_a(resample_sequence(best_seq, 16384))))
    starts.append(("rand", random_log_noise(n_primary, rng)))
    starts.append(("oneside", one_sided_template(n_primary, rng)))
    idx = 0
    while time.monotonic() < secondary_end - 30.0 and idx < len(starts):
        name, arr = starts[idx]
        rem = float(secondary_end - time.monotonic())
        alloc = min(0.11 * total, max(40.0, rem - 25.0))
        end_alt = time.monotonic() + alloc
        if end_alt < time.monotonic() + 18.0:
            break
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "start-{}".format(name),
            *optimize_start(arr, end_alt, "start-{}".format(name),
                            high_beta=25.0 * float(arr.size), quick=True)
        )
        idx += 1

    # Phase 5: perturbations of incumbent
    perturb_end = t0 + 0.93 * total
    count = 0
    while time.monotonic() < perturb_end - 35.0 and count < 4:
        p = perturb_sequence(best_seq, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25)))
        rem = float(perturb_end - time.monotonic())
        alloc = min(0.035 * total, max(25.0, rem - 20.0))
        end_p = time.monotonic() + alloc
        if end_p < time.monotonic() + 12.0:
            break
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "perturb-{}".format(count),
            *optimize_start(p, end_p, "perturb-{}".format(count),
                            high_beta=18.0 * float(p.size), quick=True)
        )
        count += 1

    # Phase 6: quick cross-length checks
    alt_lengths = [8192, 10240, 12288, 16384]
    for n_alt in alt_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
            break
        if int(best_seq.size) == n_alt:
            continue
        arr = normalize_a(resample_sequence(best_seq, n_alt))
        sc = exact_score(arr)
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update("altlen-{}-init".format(n_alt), sc, arr)
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
            best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
                "altlen-{}".format(n_alt),
                *polish_exact(arr, deadline - 24.0, "altlen-{}".format(n_alt),
                              max_no_improve=5, quick=True)
            )

    # Final polish
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        best_sc, best_seq = accepted_update(
            "final-polish",
            *polish_exact(best_seq, deadline - 8.0, "final-polish",
                          max_no_improve=20, quick=False)
        )

    out_arr = normalize_a(best_seq)
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    verified = None
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if callable(ev):
        try:
            verified = float(ev(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = None
    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_sc, verified if verified is not None else "n/a", len(out), time.monotonic() - t0))
    return out
```
